- **understand-a002**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_raft.a002 : distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols(3)` **DEFINITION:** Validity in agreement: the decided value must have been proposed by some process in the system, guaranteeing agreements are grounded in actual proposals. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-a002))
- **understand-a004**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_raft.a004 : distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols(3)` **DEFINITION:** Termination in agreement: every correct process eventually decides some value, ensuring the protocol makes progress and does not stall indefinitely. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-a004))
- **understand-a005**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_raft.a005 : distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols(3)` **DEFINITION:** Uniform agreement: all processes including those that later fail must decide on the same value, stricter than ordinary agreement which only binds correct processes. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-a005))
- **understand-a007**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_raft.a007 : distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols(3)` **DEFINITION:** Quorum in agreement: a subset of processes sufficient to guarantee agreement can be reached, typically intersecting any valid quorum to ensure overlap. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-a007))
- **understand-a008**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_raft.a008 : distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols(3)` **DEFINITION:** Threshold in agreement: the minimum number of correct processes required to guarantee safety and liveness under given failure assumptions. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-a008))
- **understand-a009**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_raft.a009 : distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols(3)` **DEFINITION:** Asymmetric agreement: one class of processes (e.g., primary) must agree while another class (e.g., backups) may reach different decisions, used in primary-backup protocols. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-a009))
- **understand-abort_reason**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.abort_reason : database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c(4), deep_isolation_level(1)` **DEFINITION:** The specific cause that precipitated a transaction's transition to the aborted_state, such as a constraint violation, deadlock detection timeout, explicit ROLLBACK request, or application-defined failure; distinct from the aborted_state itself which is the terminal condi ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-abort_reason))
- **understand-aborted_data**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.aborted_data : deep_isolation_level(1)` **DEFINITION:** The intermediate state of rows modified by a transaction that later aborted; the partial_effects_undone that existed between the first write and the rollback completion, representable as before_image/after_image pairs in the undo_log. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-aborted_data))
- **understand-aborted_state**: **CALL NUMBER:** `database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c.aborted_state : deep_isolation_level(1)` **DEFINITION:** The terminal transaction_state after rollback completes; all acquired locks are released and the transaction cannot be restarted automatically. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-aborted_state))
- **understand-abstract_factory_pattern**: **CALL NUMBER:** `software_design_patterns.abstract_factory_pattern` **DEFINITION:** Provides an interface for creating families of related objects without specifying their concrete classes, operating at the level of abstraction. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_design_patterns/understand-abstract_factory_pattern))
- **understand-access_token**: **CALL NUMBER:** `oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect.access_token : deep_oauth_2_0(1)` **DEFINITION:** OAuth 2.0 credential presented to the resource server; opaque to clients but contains or references authorization state; scoped and time-limited. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-access_token))
- **understand-access_token_expires_in**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_oauth_2_0.access_token_expires_in : oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect(10)` **DEFINITION:** Integer seconds from issuance after which the access_token is no longer valid; the resource server must reject expired tokens; returned alongside access_token in the token response. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-access_token_expires_in))
- **understand-access_token_format_type**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_oauth_2_0.access_token_format_type : oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect(10)` **DEFINITION:** Classification of the access_token internal structure: opaque (random string referencing server-side state) or structured (JWT containing claims about scope, audience, expiration embedded directly). ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-access_token_format_type))
- **understand-access_token_introspection**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_oauth_2_0.access_token_introspection : oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect(1)` **DEFINITION:** RFC 7662 mechanism by which a resource_server POSTs the access_token to the authorization_server introspection_endpoint to determine active status, scope, expiry, and metadata before serving the protected resource. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-access_token_introspection))
- **understand-access_token_presented_to**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_oauth_2_0.access_token_presented_to : oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect(10)` **DEFINITION:** The resource_server that receives and validates the access_token on each protected API call; the token is forwarded via HTTP Authorization header using the bearer scheme. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-access_token_presented_to))
- **understand-access_token_refreshed_by**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_oauth_2_0.access_token_refreshed_by : oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect(2)` **DEFINITION:** A refresh_token is a long-lived credential optionally issued alongside the access_token that allows the client to obtain a new access_token without re-engaging the resource owner; the refresh_token is tied to the same scope as the original. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-access_token_refreshed_by))
- **understand-access_token_scope**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_oauth_2_0.access_token_scope` **DEFINITION:** The specific permission set granted to the access_token; a space-delimited string drawn from the scopes requested and approved during authorization; determines what resources and actions the token authorizes. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-access_token_scope))

- **understand-attention_as_scarcy**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.attention_as_scarcy` **DEFINITION:** Attention is a finite, exhaustible resource that must be deliberately allocated; because attention is scarce, every cognitive overhead consumed by remembering, organizing, or tracking reduces capacity for deep work and flow, and PKM systems exist to offload memory and administrative cognition so that scarce attention can be directed toward m ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-attention_as_scarcy))

- **understand-author_date**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_commit_object.author_date` **DEFINITION:** Composite of author_timestamp and author_timezone; git log --date= formats this value. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-author_date))
- **understand-author_name**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_commit_object.author_name : git_internals_and_the_object_model(1)` **DEFINITION:** String component of the author field in a commit object specifying the primary creator's display name; appears before the email in the standard &lt;name&gt; &lt;email&gt; format. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-author_name))
- **understand-author_timestamp**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_commit_object.author_timestamp` **DEFINITION:** Integer Unix timestamp recording when the author created the change; represents seconds since epoch (1970-01-01 00:00:00 UTC) in the author field. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-author_timestamp))
- **understand-automatic_restart_forbidden**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.automatic_restart_forbidden` **DEFINITION:** A protocol constraint requiring that a transaction in the aborted_state cannot be re-executed automatically by the database; the application must explicitly reissue the transaction, distinguishing abort from retryable failure. Use when user asks about "backup chains", "full and incremental backups", "backup strategy", or "how incremental backups work". ([cockroachlabs/cockroach-university-assets](https://github.com/cockroachlabs/cockroach-university-assets/tree/HEAD/courses/ai-cloud-dba/configs/skilled/skills/understand-backup-chain-structure) · ★1)
- **understand-bake_phase_sequence**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_baking.bake_phase_sequence` **DEFINITION:** The ordered progression of baking stages: preheat → initial_bake_phase → final_phase → cooling_duration ([sancovp/dark-factory](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-bake_phase_sequence) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **understand-bake_time_calculation**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_baking.bake_time_calculation` **DEFINITION:** Estimation method factoring bread_volume, dough_temperature at bake, and baking_temperature to predict appropriate baking_duration for proper internal_temperature achievement. ([sancovp/dark-factory](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-bake_time_calculation) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **understand-baking**: **CALL NUMBER:** `sourdough_baking.baking : deep_fermentation(6), deep_baking(2)` **DEFINITION:** Cooking dough in hot oven transforming it into bread through heat and chemical reactions ([sancovp/dark-factory](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-baking) · ★1)

- **understand-command_message**: **CALL NUMBER:** `enterprise_integration_patterns.command_message : deep_message(9)` **DEFINITION:** A message that encodes an invocation of an operation on the receiver, carrying method name and arguments. - **understand-command_priority**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.command_priority` **DEFINITION:** A metadata field indicating the urgency or precedence level of a command message, used by schedulers and routers to order or filter command processing. - **understand-command_target**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.command_target` **DEFINITION:** The logical or physical destination endpoint to which a command message is addressed, specifying which receiver should process the invocation. - **understand-command_timeout**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.command_timeout` **DEFINITION:** The maximum duration a sender will wait for a command to complete before considering it failed; exceeding this triggers timeout handling or retry. - **understand-commit_message_encoding_encoding_header**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_commit_object.commit_message_encoding_encoding_header` **DEFINITION:** Optional header line 'encoding &lt;encoding-name&gt;' in a commit object's raw text; git stores whatever the user wrote verbatim. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-commit_message_encoding_encoding_header))
- **understand-commit_message_encoding_encoding_value**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_commit_object.commit_message_encoding_encoding_value` **DEFINITION:** The character encoding name stored in the encoding header (UTF-8, ISO-8859-1, Windows-1252, etc.); controls byte-to-character interpretation of the message bytes. - **understand-commit_message_encoding_transcoding_logic**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_commit_object.commit_message_encoding_transcoding_logic` **DEFINITION:** git's internal conversion from the stored encoding_value to the requested log_encoding_flag encoding; uses iconv-style conversion with loss handling. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-commit_message_encoding_transcoding_logic))
- **understand-commit_object**: **CALL NUMBER:** `git_internals_and_the_object_model.commit_object : deep_commit_object(8), deep_tree_object(8)` **DEFINITION:** Type-3 git object containing tree SHA, parent SHA(s), author, committer, timestamp, and message; forms the history DAG backbone. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-commit_object))
- **understand-commit_visibility_boundary**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_isolation_level.commit_visibility_boundary` **DEFINITION:** temporal threshold in read_committed: a datum is visible iff its committing_transaction committed before the reading statement started ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-commit_visibility_boundary))

- **understand-distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols_fault_tolerance**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols_fault_tolerance : deep_consensus(4)` **DEFINITION:** The ability of a distributed system to continue correct operation despite component failures including node crashes, network partitions, message loss, or Byzantine faults, typically achieved through replication and redundancy. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols_fault_tolerance))
- **understand-distributed_transaction**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.distributed_transaction` **DEFINITION:** A transaction that spans multiple independent services or database_per_service instances, requiring coordination to maintain atomicity across nodes where each participant may reside in a different process or machine. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-distributed_transaction))
- **understand-distributed_transaction**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.distributed_transaction` **DEFINITION:** A transaction that spans multiple independent nodes or services, requiring coordination across participants to ensure atomicity, typically via two-phase commit, three-phase commit, or compensating transaction patterns. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-distributed_transaction))
- **understand-djt_bounding_range**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.djt_bounding_range` **DEFINITION:** The closed interval [min_delay, previous_delay times decorrelation_factor] from which the uniform random sample is drawn to compute the next current_delay; bounds the worst-case wait while allowing history-dependent growth. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-djt_bounding_range))
- **understand-djt_random_uniform_sample**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.djt_random_uniform_sample` **DEFINITION:** The uniform random draw within the bounding range that produces the new current_delay value; the randomness decorrelates this thread's retry timing from concurrent threads. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-djt_random_uniform_sample))
- **understand-djt_wave_attenuation**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.djt_wave_attenuation` **DEFINITION:** The emergent property that decorrelated jitter reduces the probability of simultaneous retries among competing threads; achieved through per-thread history-dependent ranges that diverge over time. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-djt_wave_attenuation))

- **understand-dough_handling**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.dough_handling` **DEFINITION:** Techniques for manipulating dough during shaping including stretching, folding, and rolling that develop gluten structure and determine final loaf form. ([sancovp/dark-factory](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-dough_handling) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **understand-dps_001**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_microservices_archit.dps_001 : software_architecture_patterns_and_styles(15), deep_event_driven_archite(9)` **DEFINITION:** database_per_service is a data management pattern in microservices where each service owns and manages its own database schema or instance, enforcing loose coupling between services through physical data isolation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-dps_001))
- **understand-dps_002**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_microservices_archit.dps_002` **DEFINITION:** schema_isolation means each service's database exposes only the data and operations relevant to its bounded context, hiding internal structures from other services. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-dps_002))
- **understand-dps_003**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_microservices_archit.dps_003 : software_architecture_patterns_and_styles(4)` **DEFINITION:** instance_isolation means each service runs its own database server process or cluster, with separate credentials, ports, and storage, preventing any service from accessing another's data store directly. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-dps_003))
- **understand-dps_006**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_microservices_archit.dps_006 : software_architecture_patterns_and_styles(15), deep_event_driven_archite(9)` **DEFINITION:** data_duplication is the consequence of database_per_service where the same logical entity may be materialized in multiple service databases, kept synchronized through application-level events. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-dps_006))
- **understand-dps_014**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_microservices_archit.dps_014 : deep_event_driven_archite(9), software_architecture_patterns_and_styles(8)` **DEFINITION:** eventual_consistency_model is the consistency guarantee that results from database_per_service: writes are immediately durable in one service's database, and other services receive updates asynchronously via event_streaming or message_queue. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-dps_014))
- **understand-du_match**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tagged_union_type.du_match` **DEFINITION:** The elimination form: a function that, given a discriminated union value, dispatches on its du_tag to apply the appropriate handler to the du_payload, yielding a uniform result type. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_type_systems_for_working_programmers/understand-du_match))
- **understand-du_payload**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tagged_union_type.du_payload` **DEFINITION:** The data value carried by a specific variant of a discriminated union. Only one payload is live at a time; the tag selects which one. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_type_systems_for_working_programmers/understand-du_payload))
- **understand-du_tag**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tagged_union_type.du_tag` **DEFINITION:** A value-level or type-level label that uniquely identifies which variant of a discriminated union is active in a given value. The tag is the first-class evidence of variant membership. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_type_systems_for_working_programmers/understand-du_tag))
- **understand-du_variant**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tagged_union_type.du_variant` **DEFINITION:** A named constituent of a discriminated union, pairing a du_tag with a du_payload type. Each variant represents one summand in the sum type. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_type_systems_for_working_programmers/understand-du_variant))
- **understand-dur_recovery_manager**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.dur_recovery_manager` **DEFINITION:** The subsystem that enforces durability by reconstructing the committed database state from transaction_log after a crash; scans log forward to redo committed changes and backward to undo uncommitted ones. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-dur_recovery_manager))
- **understand-dur_write_ahead_logging**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.dur_write_ahead_logging` **DEFINITION:** A protocol mandating that log records describing modifications be flushed to durable_storage before the corresponding data pages are written to disk; foundational mechanism for both atomicity and durability. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-dur_write_ahead_logging))
- **understand-dutch_oven_bake_sequence**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_baking.dutch_oven_bake_sequence` **DEFINITION:** Baking protocol for covered dutch oven comprising initial lid-on phase for steam retention followed by lid_removal phase for crust coloring. ([sancovp/dark-factory](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-dutch_oven_bake_sequence) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **understand-hb_release_semantics**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_happens_before_relat.hb_release_semantics` **DEFINITION:** A memory ordering guarantee where all prior memory operations become visible before the release operation; paired with acquire to establish synchronization. - **understand-hb_transitive_closure**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_happens_before_relat.hb_transitive_closure` **DEFINITION:** The transitive property of happens-before: if A happens-before B and B happens-before C, then A happens-before C.
